独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 39682172014 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

F2 · 认证指引未记录公开的 preparation/retryPreparation

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

E13 把 preparation: 'preparing'|'ready'|'failed' 和 retryPreparation 暴露为 AuthenticationContextValue 的公开成员；但 E43 的 SKILL.md 客户端 API 列表仍写 useAuthentication() 返回 { client, session, isPending, refresh }，E14 的 client-session-and-pages.md 会话章节也只讲 session/isPending/refresh/client，均未提 preparation 或 realtime 失败态，认证技能目录全文检索也无相关内容。E44 显示冻结应用必须自行实现 failed 监听与重试，印证仅按现有指引无法得知该公开入口。文档在冻结仓内可直接读取，非环境或工厂覆盖问题；补充指引即可，属 guidance-gap。

  • 职责与公开约定：E13：AuthenticationContextValue 明确公开 preparation 的三态与 retryPreparation()，这是页面可依赖的公开契约；指引应覆盖它。 E13
  • 实际行为证据：E14 的会话章节（L1-L30）只列 session/isPending/refresh/client；E43 的 SKILL.md L44-L50 也只列四项。两处均无 preparation、retryPreparation 或 10 秒失败说明，文档实际内容与候选描述一致。 E14 E43
  • 应用接入与业务边界：E44：应用 SessionPreparation 直接读取 preparation/retryPreparation 并实现 failed 监听与重试，说明该公开入口是实际集成点；文档缺失迫使应用读实现或自行补 socket 监听。 E44
  • 环境因素：E14 是仓库内冻结的技能文档，本地可读且不依赖外部服务；未发现环境不可用或外部凭据导致缺页，缺失内容是文档本身。 E14
  • 工厂因素：E43 属于随包发布的 authentication Skill 内容，未见工厂提示、注入配置或模板覆盖修改它；遗漏可直接在 Skill 文档中定位，不是工厂材料缺失。 E43 E14
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：检查 authentication Skill 的 SKILL.md 与 references，除 E13 的 d.ts 注释外没有其他 preparation/retryPreparation 使用说明（E14/E43）；现有文档能力不足以替代指引。 E13 E14 E43

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

auth-provider.d.ts 把 preparation: 'preparing' | 'ready' | 'failed' 和 retryPreparation 暴露为 AuthenticationContextValue，但 client-session-and-pages.md 的“Reading the session”只讲 session、isPending、refresh 和 client，没有说明 10 秒失败和恢复入口。

实际影响
Agent 容易把 getSession 完成当成应用已可用，遇到 Unable to connect 时只能读实现或自行猜监听 realtimeClientToken，增加排查和重复接入代码。
建议改法
在 client-session-and-pages.md 增加 preparation/retryPreparation 段落，给出失败态渲染和重试示例，并从认证 Skill 的会话章节链接过去；同时说明自动重试的边界。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
应用按 client-session-and-pages.md 使用 useAuthentication() 处理会话和页面守卫。
应有能力 / 行为
指引列出 preparation 状态、失败原因和 retryPreparation 的推荐用法。
实际观察
公开类型有 preparation/retryPreparation，但指引只覆盖 session/isPending/refresh/client，未提 realtime 准备失败。
应用绕行与代价
应用读取插件源码后自行实现 preparation=failed 的监听和重试。
改进后的验收标准
按更新后的指引即可实现失败态提示和重试，不依赖阅读 dist 实现；示例应覆盖 preparing、ready、failed 三种状态。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 2 条
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14E13

AuthenticationContextValue 公开 preparation: 'preparing'|'ready'|'failed'、retryPreparation()、refresh() 和 isPending，说明失败恢复是公开契约的一部分。

export interface AuthenticationContextValue {
    readonly client: AuthClient;
    readonly session: AuthClient['$Infer']['Session'] | null;
    readonly isPending: boolean;
    readonly refresh: () => Promise<void>;
    /** First realtime identity preparation; independent of the session query. */
    readonly preparation: 'preparing' | 'ready' | 'failed';
    readonly retryPreparation: () => void;
}
export declare function AuthenticationProvider({ children, }: PropsWithChildren): ReactElement;
export declare function useAuthentication(): AuthenticationContextValue;
export declare function useAuthenticationClient(): AuthClient;
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md:1–30E14

指引只说明 session/isPending/refresh/client、退出和守卫，未出现 preparation 或 retryPreparation，也未描述 realtime 准备超时的失败态。

# Client session and pages

## Reading the session

`useAuthentication()` works anywhere under the application shell:

```tsx
import { useAuthentication } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/client';

export function Greeting(): ReactElement | null {
  const { session, isPending } = useAuthentication();
  if (isPending || !session) return null;
  return <span>{session.user.name}</span>;
}
```

- `session` is `null` while anonymous; `session.user` has `id`, `name`,
  `email`, `username`, `image`, and `emailVerified`.
- `isPending` is true until the first `getSession()` completes and during any
  `refresh()`. Render nothing or a placeholder while pending; do not treat
  pending as anonymous.
- `refresh()` re-reads the session from the server. Call it after any action
  that changes the session outside the provided hooks.
- `client` is the Better Auth client. Every Better Auth client plugin
  configured in `client/config/auth.ts` is available on it.

The provider does not poll and does not listen for revocation. A session
revoked on the server is noticed at the next `refresh()` or when an API call
returns `401`; treat that `401` as "sign in again", not as an application
error.
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][指引缺失] 认证指引未记录公开的 preparation/retryPreparation

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.10）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

- 职责与公开约定：E13：AuthenticationContextValue 明确公开 preparation 的三态与 retryPreparation()，这是页面可依赖的公开契约；指引应覆盖它。；证据：E13（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14）
- 实际行为证据：E14 的会话章节（L1-L30）只列 session/isPending/refresh/client；E43 的 SKILL.md L44-L50 也只列四项。两处均无 preparation、retryPreparation 或 10 秒失败说明，文档实际内容与候选描述一致。；证据：E14（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md:1–30）、E43（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:44–50）

轻微易用性改进上游未复核候选反馈：证据不足原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · 认证 realtime 准备超时后不会因晚到的连接恢复

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

E12 确认 provider 在 10s 超时后取消监听并发布 failed，晚到 onOpen 不会恢复；但候选把用户影响写成“必须手动 Retry”，与冻结应用的现有补偿矛盾：app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx 在 failed 时订阅 realtime onOpen 并在 1.5s 后自动 retryPreparation，autoRetries=0 时仍显示 Loading（E44）。两轮 QA 的阻断页还可能由 agent-browser 丢失 WebSocket 常量（E35/E37）或工厂录制状态缺失（E34）解释，未证明真的发生过 >10s 的晚到握手。公开 preparation/retryPreparation 已允许页面自动恢复（E13）。因此代码事实成立，但本条的用户影响与归因前提不足，不能据此认定框架必须改动。

  • 职责与公开约定：E13 只公开 preparation/retryPreparation 与 failed 状态，恢复由调用方触发；没有承诺晚到连接自动恢复。框架职责边界是暴露状态，页面决定重试策略，建议本身不足以构成违约。 E13
  • 实际行为证据：E12 L74-L97：onOpen 成功后 clearTimeout、unsubscribe 并 publish ready；定时器到点则 cancelled=true、unsubscribe、publish failed。之后 socket 打开时回调已被注销且 cancelled 阻止发布，provider 不会自动回 ready；只有 retryPreparation 递增 retry 才重跑 effect。 E12
  • 应用接入与业务边界：E44：应用 SessionPreparation 在 preparation=failed 时订阅 realtimeClientToken 的 onOpen 并自动 retryPreparation，首次失败仍显示 Preparing；晚到连接可由 onOpen 恢复，故“用户会停在全屏错误、必须手动 Retry”在本应用不成立，应用也已正确装配公开 API。 E44
  • 环境因素：E35/E37 显示 QA 需在页面 eval 中补 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED，录制或刷新后常量丢失；E34 显示 FACTORY_BROWSER_RECORDING_STATE 缺失。E40/E41 的 Unable to connect 可能是包装器/录制环境导致，不能当作 >10s 握手的证据。 E35 E37 E34 E40 E41
  • 工厂因素：E34 的录制状态缺失与 E35 的 record start 重置页面上下文属于工厂浏览器工具链问题；这些操作会丢失页面注入状态并复现失败页，说明观察到的阻断不能直接归因于插件超时。 E34 E35
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：E13 的 retryPreparation 与 E44 的 onOpen+retry 组合已能实现自动恢复；冻结应用就是现成例子，说明公开能力已存在，缺的是由谁承担重试策略，而非无恢复入口。 E13 E44

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

auth-provider.js 在 PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS=10_000 后把 preparation 发布为 failed 并 unsubscribe，不再监听迟到的 onOpen；随后整个应用显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared”。公开的 retryPreparation() 可恢复，但首轮刷新和 B03 都出现该阻断页，应用还在 session-preparation.tsx 里额外监听 realtimeClientToken 自动重试一次。

实际影响
硬刷新或重连时，如果 socket 在 10 秒后才就绪，用户会停在无法进入应用的全屏错误，必须手动点 Retry；应用必须写补偿逻辑才能避免误判会话失败。
建议改法
超时后保留一次晚到的 onOpen 监听，或在 failed 状态下按退避自动重连；至少把 preparation/retryPreparation 纳入公开客户端状态机并让页面可选择自动恢复。修改点应在 @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication 的 AuthenticationProvider，不应要求每个业务应用自定义 socket 监听。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
整页刷新或重新建立 realtime 连接时，握手超过 10 秒才成功。
应有能力 / 行为
socket 晚到后 AuthenticationProvider 自动从 preparing/failed 进入 ready；或至少自动重试并在文档中给出处理方式。
实际观察
定时器到点即发布 failed 并注销 onOpen，之后连接成功也不会恢复；两轮 QA 都看到全屏 Unable to connect，需要 Retry 或应用侧补偿。
应用绕行与代价
应用 session-preparation.tsx 在 preparation=failed 时监听 realtimeClientToken，并在 socket 打开后自动重试一次；用户也可手动点 Retry。
改进后的验收标准
模拟 realtime 握手在 10 秒后成功，刷新页面应自动进入应用且不需要手动 Retry；retryPreparation 仍应可重复调用并最终收敛到 ready。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:7–98E12

PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS=10_000；onOpen 取消定时器并发布 ready，定时器到点则 cancel、unsubscribe 并发布 failed；成功后安装的 onOpen 在超时后已被注销，不会恢复。

const PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
const AuthenticationContext = createContext(undefined);
export function AuthenticationProvider({ children, }) {
    const client = useService(authenticationClientToken);
    const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
    const [isPending, setPendingState] = useState(true);
    const refreshAttemptRef = useRef(0);
    const refresh = async () => {
        const attempt = ++refreshAttemptRef.current;
        setPendingState(true);
        try {
            const result = await client.getSession();
            if (attempt === refreshAttemptRef.current)
                setSession(result.data);
        }
        finally {
            if (attempt === refreshAttemptRef.current)
                setPendingState(false);
        }
    };
    useEffect(() => {
        void refresh();
        return () => {
            refreshAttemptRef.current += 1;
        };
    }, [client]);
    const services = useClientApplication().services;
    const sessionKey = JSON.stringify([
        session?.user.id ?? null,
        session?.session.id ?? null,
    ]);
    const signedIn = session !== null;
    const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
    const retryPreparation = useCallback(() => setRetry((value) => value + 1), []);
    const [prepared, setPrepared] = useState();
    const previousSessionRef = useRef(undefined);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isPending)
            return;
        const realtime = services.has(realtimeClientToken)
            ? services.resolveIfCreated(realtimeClientToken)
            : undefined;
        const changed = previousSessionRef.current !== sessionKey;
        const hadSession = previousSessionRef.current !== undefined;
        previousSessionRef.current = sessionKey;
        if (!signedIn) {
            // Sign-out must also remove the identity from an existing connection.
            if (changed && hadSession)
                realtime?.reconnect();
            return;
        }
        if (!changed &&
            prepared?.sessionKey === sessionKey &&
            prepared.retry === retry &&
            prepared.status === 'ready')
            return;
        const publish = (status) => {
            // Preparation is an external connection lifecycle, independent of rendering.
            // eslint-disable-next-line @eslint-react/set-state-in-effect
            setPrepared({ sessionKey, retry, status });
        };
        if (!realtime?.active) {
            publish('ready');
            return;
        }
        let cancelled = false;
        publish('preparing');
        const unsubscribe = realtime.onOpen(() => {
            // Authorization's synchronous onOpen invalidation must finish first,
            // regardless of listener registration order.
            queueMicrotask(() => {
                if (cancelled)
                    return;
                cancelled = true;
                clearTimeout(timer);
                unsubscribe();
                publish('ready');
            });
        });
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            cancelled = true;
            unsubscribe();
            publish('failed');
        }, PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS);
        // Even cookie startup reconnects: connected alone does not prove identity.
        realtime.reconnect();
        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
            clearTimeout(timer);
            unsubscribe();
        };
    }, [isPending, sessionKey, signedIn, services, retry]);
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14E13

AuthenticationContextValue 公开 preparation: 'preparing'|'ready'|'failed'、retryPreparation()、refresh() 和 isPending，说明失败恢复是公开契约的一部分。

export interface AuthenticationContextValue {
    readonly client: AuthClient;
    readonly session: AuthClient['$Infer']['Session'] | null;
    readonly isPending: boolean;
    readonly refresh: () => Promise<void>;
    /** First realtime identity preparation; independent of the session query. */
    readonly preparation: 'preparing' | 'ready' | 'failed';
    readonly retryPreparation: () => void;
}
export declare function AuthenticationProvider({ children, }: PropsWithChildren): ReactElement;
export declare function useAuthentication(): AuthenticationContextValue;
export declare function useAuthenticationClient(): AuthClient;

上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][易用性改进] 认证 realtime 准备超时后不会因晚到的连接恢复

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.10）

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

E12 确认 provider 在 10s 超时后取消监听并发布 failed，晚到 onOpen 不会恢复；但候选把用户影响写成“必须手动 Retry”，与冻结应用的现有补偿矛盾：app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx 在 failed 时订阅 realtime onOpen 并在 1.5s 后自动 retryPreparation，autoRetries=0 时仍显示 Loading（E44）。两轮 QA 的阻断页还可能由 agent-browser 丢失 WebSocket 常量（E35/E37）或工厂录制状态缺失（E34）解释，未证明真的发生过 >10s 的晚到握手。公开 preparation/retryPreparation 已允许页面自动恢复（E13）。因此代码事实成立，但本条的用户影响与归因前提不足，不能据此认定框架必须改动。

- 职责与公开约定：E13 只公开 preparation/retryPreparation 与 failed 状态，恢复由调用方触发；没有承诺晚到连接自动恢复。框架职责边界是暴露状态，页面决定重试策略，建议本身不足以构成违约。；证据：E13（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14）
- 实际行为证据：E12 L74-L97：onOpen 成功后 clearTimeout、unsubscribe 并 publish ready；定时器到点则 cancelled=true、unsubscribe、publish failed。之后 socket 打开时回调已被注销且 cancelled 阻止发布，provider 不会自动回 ready；只有 retryPreparation 递增 retry 才重跑 effect。；证据：E12（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:7–98）
- 应用接入与业务边界：E44：应用 SessionPreparation 在 preparation=failed 时订阅 realtimeClientToken 的 onOpen 并自动 retryPreparation，首次失败仍显示 Preparing；晚到连接可由 onOpen 恢复，故“用户会停在全屏错误、必须手动 Retry”在本应用不成立，应用也已正确装配公开 API。；证据：E44（app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:17–58）
- 环境因素：E35/E37 显示 QA 需在页面 eval 中补 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED，录制或刷新后常量丢失；E34 显示 FACTORY_BROWSER_RECORDING_STATE 缺失。E40/E41 的 Unable to connect 可能是包装器/录制环境导致，不能当作 >10s 握手的证据。；证据：E35（artifacts/agent-history/verify-2/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0009.txt:407–412）、E37（artifacts/agent-history/verify-2/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0010.txt:1640–1645）、E34（artifacts/agent-history/verify-1/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0001.txt:582–591）、E40（artifacts/agent-history/verify-1/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0002.txt:94–96）、E41（artifacts/agent-history/verify-1/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0006.txt:290–294）
- 工厂因素：E34 的录制状态缺失与 E35 的 record start 重置页面上下文属于工厂浏览器工具链问题；这些操作会丢失页面注入状态并复现失败页，说明观察到的阻断不能直接归因于插件超时。；证据：E34（artifacts/agent-history/verify-1/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0001.txt:582–591）、E35（artifacts/agent-history/verify-2/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0009.txt:407–412）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：E13 的 retryPreparation 与 E44 的 onOpen+retry 组合已能实现自动恢复；冻结应用就是现成例子，说明公开能力已存在，缺的是由谁承担重试策略，而非无恢复入口。；证据：E13（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14）、E44（app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:17–58）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

auth-provider.js 在 PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS=10_000 后把 preparation 发布为 failed 并 unsubscribe，不再监听迟到的 onOpen；随后整个应用显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared”。公开的 retryPreparation() 可恢复，但首轮刷新和 B03 都出现该阻断页，应用还在 session-preparation.tsx 里额外监听 realtimeClientToken 自动重试一次。

**影响**：硬刷新或重连时，如果 socket 在 10 秒后才就绪，用户会停在无法进入应用的全屏错误，必须手动点 Retry；应用必须写补偿逻辑才能避免误判会话失败。

**建议改动**：超时后保留一次晚到的 onOpen 监听，或在 failed 状态下按退避自动重连；至少把 preparation/retryPreparation 纳入公开客户端状态机并让页面可选择自动恢复。修改点应在 @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication 的 AuthenticationProvider，不应要求每个业务应用自定义 socket 监听。

**验收标准**：模拟 realtime 握手在 10 秒后成功，刷新页面应自动进入应用且不需要手动 Retry；retryPreparation 仍应可重复调用并最终收敛到 ready。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:7–98` — PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS=10_000；onOpen 取消定时器并发布 ready，定时器到点则 cancel、unsubscribe 并发布 failed；成功后安装的 onOpen 在超时后已被注销，不会恢复。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.d.ts:3–14` — AuthenticationContextValue 公开 preparation: 'preparing'|'ready'|'failed'、retryPreparation()、refresh() 和 isPending，说明失败恢复是公开契约的一部分。
- `artifacts/agent-history/verify-1/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0002.txt:94–96` — 首轮 QA 在 /main 等待后读到全屏 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared. Retry to enter the application, or sign out.”，并保存了 realtime-error 截图。
- `artifacts/agent-history/verify-1/browser-acceptance/agent-browser-acceptance.jsonl/part-0006.txt:290–294` — 首轮 B03 在停用后刷新仍然得到同一 “Unable to connect” 全屏错误，说明失败发生在会话/realtime 准备阶段而不是设置持久化本身。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 39682172014 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。

重要实现缺陷候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed原评审：已解决
F3 · 应用父页面漏写 <Outlet /> 导致执行记录子页不渲染

Agent / 业务实现 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

首轮 Scheduler 列表页声明了嵌套的 /scheduler/history 路由，但父页面没有渲染 <Outlet />；点击 View history 后 URL 改变而内容仍是计划列表，B01 因此失败。page.md 和 child-routes.md 都明确要求父页面自行放置 Outlet。修复后列表页在 PageContainer 内加入 Outlet，第二轮记录页正常显示。

实际影响
这是应用页面组合错误，首轮 B01 失败并触发修复；不是 @nocobase/app-client 的路由解析缺陷，也不应据此给框架路由能力扣分。
建议改法
保持应用页面在 PageContainer 末尾渲染 <Outlet />；应用测试应覆盖直接导航到子路由并断言子页面内容，而不只断言路由元数据。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
首轮点击 Scheduler 的 View history，导航到嵌套执行记录路由。
应有能力 / 行为
父页面在 Outlet 位置渲染执行记录子页。
实际观察
地址栏变为子路由，但页面内容仍是计划列表，执行记录不可见。
应用绕行与代价
在父页面 PageContainer 末尾加入 <Outlet />；第二轮通过并显示执行记录。
改进后的验收标准
从列表点击 View history 或直接打开子路由时，执行记录内容出现在父页面 Outlet 位置，刷新后仍可显示。

实现者过程与运行背景 · 3 个过程问题 / 2 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

Repaired the two observed defects. The Scheduler page never rendered its child route outlet, so View history changed the URL but kept showing the schedule list; the page now renders its outlet and the history page is a covering RouteChildPage. Session preparation treated a connection that opened after its 10s timeout as terminal, so a hard refresh could leave the 'Unable to connect' screen up until someone pressed Retry; the shell now retries once silently and keeps listening for a late connection. Verified with unit/component tests, a production build and Playwright runs against the built app (normal reload, 20s and 30s realtime outages, history route before and after a hard refresh).

问题 01 · verification

View history changed the URL but the execution records never rendered

本任务已处理

Clicking the plan's View history navigated to /main/settings/scheduler/<id>, but the main content stayed the schedule list; reloading that address did too.

原因（实现者自述）
client/pages/scheduler/index.tsx rendered only the list and had no <Outlet/>, so the settingsSchedulerDetail child route could never mount. The history page itself also rendered no covering layer for a route child.
处理方式
Added <Outlet/> to the scheduler page (with a comment) and wrapped the history page in RouteChildPage with its own PageContainer. Rewrote the component test to render the child route through the parent route, so the outlet is covered and the list is asserted to stay mounted underneath. Verified against the built app: View history shows the definition, cron, 19 execution records and the list stays mounted; the history page also survives a hard refresh.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 02 · verification

Hard refresh showed 'Unable to connect' until Retry was pressed

本任务已处理

After disabling the plan, a hard refresh of /main/settings/scheduler rendered 'Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared' and only a manual Retry restored the page. The disabled state itself persisted correctly.

原因（实现者自述）
Authentication preparation waits 10s for the realtime connection, then publishes 'failed' and unsubscribes its onOpen listener. A connection that opens later (or a handshake that is simply slow) is ignored forever, so the shell shows a permanent failure screen for a transient problem. Reproduced with Playwright by holding the /ws handshake: the failure appeared at ~10s and never cleared even after the connection became possible.
处理方式
client/routing/session-preparation.tsx now retries the preparation once automatically (staying on the loading screen, since a late connection is not a failure) and keeps an onOpen listener while preparation is failed, so a connection that opens later retries and mounts the page without a click. The localized retry/sign-out screen still appears when that retry also fails and remains the escape hatch. Documented in .agents/rules/shell.md and covered by new session-preparation tests. Browser-verified: a 20s realtime outage recovers at ~20s with no error screen; a 30s outage reports the failure at ~21s and then recovers by itself at ~30s; normal reloads load in ~150ms with no failure.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 03 · verification

No create/edit entry on the Todos page, so the No-to-Yes transition cannot be staged by hand

未解决

The QA pass could only confirm the final state after a run: the seeded overdue todo was already Expired=Yes and there was no way to add a fresh overdue todo.

原因（实现者自述）
The task asked for a minimal read-only list plus one seeded record of each kind; no authoring UI was requested, and the scheduler fires every 30s so a seeded overdue row is marked before an observer can watch the transition.
处理方式
Retained as is: there is no explicit requirement to author todos, and adding a create/edit surface would exceed 'only implement the stated requirements'. The seeded rows, the toggle, and the execution history (which records each run's marked count) remain the evidence that the schedule runs.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · framework

Session preparation treats a slow or late realtime handshake as terminal

可自动化

app-plugin-authentication's preparation publishes 'failed' after 10s and removes its onOpen listener, so a websocket that opens afterwards is never used. Every application shell then has to compensate: the failure screen is permanent even though the connection later succeeds, and a hard refresh can block the page behind a Retry click.

建议改动

Keep the onOpen listener after the timeout, or allow a late open to complete the preparation (publish ready) instead of requiring a reconnect through retryPreparation. Applications would then not need shell-level retry logic.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · template-tests

A page that owns child routes can silently lose them by omitting its Outlet

可自动化

The child route in client/pages/settings/routes.ts mounted nowhere while the URL changed and the parent list stayed visible; nothing in the template's tests noticed. A component test that renders the child route through its parent route (as the repaired test now does) catches this, but only if it is written that way.

建议改动

In the template's route/page guidance or a scaffolded test, render child routes through the parent route and assert both the child content and the parent staying mounted, rather than rendering the child page directly.

来源：Agent · retro.json

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

在既有 Scheduler 管理界面声明“检查过期待办”计划，指向一个真实可执行目标，并保留执行结果入口。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
Scheduler 负责定义/同步计划、注册目标类型、触发执行、持久化 occurrence 与暴露查询；应用负责目标业务逻辑、cron 和业务幂等。
推荐使用方式
Provider 的 register()/boot() 中调用 schedulerServiceToken.defineSchedule()，并用 registerTarget() 注册 app.* 目标；短任务直接返回 completed/succeeded。
Agent 实际使用
应用在 TodoProvider.boot() 注册 app.check-overdue-todos 目标并定义每 30 秒计划；目标调用同一个 Todo service 的 markOverdue()，没有直接写表。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E1 E5 E6 E19
一次真实调度后记录显示结果；再次执行同一目标时业务结果保持幂等，启停状态刷新后仍保存。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
Scheduler 负责 occurrence 去重/终态、启停落库和记录查询；应用负责筛选条件与“已过期不再重复标记”的业务幂等。
推荐使用方式
用 occurrence id 和 TERMINAL 状态避免重复执行；用 setEnabled()/listOccurrences() 读写启停与历史；业务更新使用明确的非空 filter。
Agent 实际使用
第二轮 QA 观察到 23:32:30 与 23:33:00 的两次真实运行，记录 succeeded（marked:1 后 marked:0），待办总数仍为 3；Paused 在刷新后保持。

E1 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/services/scheduler.js:14–59

DefaultSchedulerService 的 defineSchedule/registerTarget/list/listOccurrences：计划只进入 manifest，目标注册返回受限 completion 句柄，列表和 occurrence 查询由 service 统一暴露。

    defineSchedule(definition) {
        this.manifest.push(Object.freeze({ definition: buildScheduleDefinition(definition) }));
    }
    registerTarget(target) {
        this.targets.register(target);
        const { type } = target;
        return {
            type,
            reportCompletion: (occurrenceId, reference, completion) => this.occurrences.complete(occurrenceId, reference, completion, type),
        };
    }
    async list() {
        const [records, targets] = await Promise.all([
            this.store.list(),
            this.store.listTargets(),
        ]);
        const byId = new Map(targets.map((target) => [target.id, target]));
        return Promise.all(records.map(async (record) => {
            const target = byId.get(record.id);
            return {
                ...record,
                targetState: target
                    ? ((await this.targets.describe(target.type, target.config))
                        .state ?? 'invalid')
                    : 'missing',
                targetSummary: target
                    ? await this.targets.describe(target.type, target.config)
                    : { targetLabel: record.targetType, state: 'missing' },
            };
        }));
    }
    /** One schedule with the same projection as `list()`, or `undefined` when this application has no such schedule. */
    async get(id) {
        return (await this.list()).find((entry) => entry.id === id);
    }
    async listOccurrences(scheduleId, range = {}) {
        const occurrences = await this.store.listOccurrences(scheduleId, range);
        return occurrences.map((occurrence) => {
            const href = occurrence.target.reference
                ? this.targets.referenceHref(occurrence.target.type, occurrence.target.reference)
                : undefined;
            return {
                ...occurrence,
                target: { ...occurrence.target, ...(href ? { href } : {}) },
            };
        });

E2 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/occurrences.js:1–72

TERMINAL 包含 succeeded/failed/skipped/cancelled/timed_out/triggered；start() 对 waiting 或终态 occurrence 返回 noop，插入竞争也返回 noop，避免重复执行。

const TERMINAL = new Set([
    'succeeded',
    'failed',
    'skipped',
    'cancelled',
    'timed_out',
    'triggered',
]);
const MAX_SUMMARY_BYTES = 16_384;
export class ScheduleOccurrenceError extends Error {
    code;
    constructor(code, message) {
        super(message);
        this.code = code;
        this.name = 'ScheduleOccurrenceError';
    }
}
export class ScheduleOccurrenceStore {
    database;
    constructor(database) {
        this.database = database;
    }
    async start(context, definitionHash, targetType, scheduledAt) {
        const now = new Date();
        const existing = await this.find(context.occurrenceId);
        if (existing) {
            if (existing.status === 'waiting' || TERMINAL.has(existing.status))
                return 'noop';
            const updated = await this.database
                .query()
                .updateTable('schedule_occurrences')
                .set({
                status: 'running',
                executionCount: existing.executionCount + 1,
                lastStartedAt: now,
                updatedAt: now,
            })
                .where('id', '=', context.occurrenceId)
                .where('status', 'in', ['pending', 'running'])
                .execute();
            if ((updated.updatedCount ?? 0) === 0) {
                const current = await this.required(context.occurrenceId);
                if (current.status === 'waiting' || TERMINAL.has(current.status))
                    return 'noop';
            }
            return 'start';
        }
        const inserted = await this.insertOnce({
            id: context.occurrenceId,
            scheduleId: context.scheduleId,
            definitionHash,
            status: 'running',
            reason: null,
            targetType,
            targetReferenceType: null,
            targetReferenceId: null,
            targetReceipt: null,
            resultSummary: null,
            executionCount: 1,
            scheduledAt: scheduledAt ?? null,
            startedAt: now,
            acceptedAt: null,
            lastStartedAt: now,
            lastObservedAt: null,
            finishedAt: null,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
        });
        // Losing the insert to a concurrent delivery of the same firing means that
        // delivery runs it.
        return inserted ? 'start' : 'noop';
    }

原文件 SHA-256：392a5f060cc61804e73b02e79a221ecacc3595bc261e987ecf29fbfadff141c3

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/store.js:188–213

setEnabled(false) 先 removeJob 再写 enabled=false/nextRunAt=null；启用时按剩余 runLimit 重新 applyRule 并持久化。

    async setEnabled(id, enabled) {
        const definition = await this.database
            .query()
            .selectFrom('schedule_definitions')
            .selectAll()
            .where('id', '=', id)
            .where('appName', '=', this.appName)
            .executeTakeFirst();
        if (!definition)
            throw new ScheduleNotFoundError(id);
        if (!enabled) {
            // The handler stays registered, so another instance that enables the
            // schedule again finds this instance able to run it.
            await this.executor.removeJob(id);
            await this.updateDefinition(id, { enabled: false, nextRunAt: null });
            return;
        }
        const spec = specOfRow(definition);
        const limit = nullableNumber(definition.runLimit);
        // Re-adding a rule restarts BullMQ's count, so it carries what is left.
        const appliedLimit = limit === null ? null : limit - Number(definition.runCount ?? 0);
        const nextRunAt = definition.lifecycleState === 'active'
            ? await this.applyRule(spec, appliedLimit, true)
            : null;
        await this.updateDefinition(id, { enabled: true, appliedLimit, nextRunAt });
    }

原文件 SHA-256：387d2d429a9192f090ee6d4c712fdf1398baa4917c36641de06ced007699e473

E4 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/store.js:251–307

listOccurrences 校验 schedule 属于当前 app，按 startedAt/id 倒序分页并投影状态、时间、targetReceipt/resultSummary，供管理界面展示执行结果。

    async listOccurrences(scheduleId, range = {}) {
        const owned = await this.database
            .query()
            .selectFrom('schedule_definitions')
            .select('id')
            .where('id', '=', scheduleId)
            .where('appName', '=', this.appName)
            .exists();
        if (!owned)
            return [];
        const rows = await this.database
            .query()
            .selectFrom('schedule_occurrences')
            .selectAll()
            .where('scheduleId', '=', scheduleId)
            .orderBy('startedAt', 'desc')
            .orderBy('id', 'desc')
            .limit(range.limit ?? 100)
            .offset(range.offset ?? 0)
            .execute();
        return rows.map((row) => ({
            id: String(row.id),
            scheduleId: String(row.scheduleId),
            status: row.status,
            ...(typeof row.reason === 'string' ? { reason: row.reason } : {}),
            executionCount: Number(row.executionCount),
            startedAt: dateValue(row.startedAt) ?? '',
            ...(row.acceptedAt
                ? { acceptedAt: dateValue(row.acceptedAt) }
                : {}),
            ...(row.finishedAt
                ? { finishedAt: dateValue(row.finishedAt) }
                : {}),
            ...(row.targetReceipt
                ? {
                    targetReceipt: jsonObject(row.targetReceipt),
                }
                : {}),
            ...(row.resultSummary
                ? {
                    resultSummary: jsonObject(row.resultSummary),
                }
                : {}),
            target: {
                type: String(row.targetType),
                ...(typeof row.targetReferenceType === 'string' &&
                    typeof row.targetReferenceId === 'string'
                    ? {
                        reference: {
                            type: row.targetReferenceType,
                            id: row.targetReferenceId,
                        },
                    }
                    : {}),
            },
        }));
    }

原文件 SHA-256：387d2d429a9192f090ee6d4c712fdf1398baa4917c36641de06ced007699e473

E5 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:10–44

Skill 明确 Scheduler 用于管理员可见的 UI 可观察任务，业务逻辑/Provider 归应用；并给出 defineSchedule 与 registerTarget 的开发循环和验证步骤。

Use the installed version's public exports, without importing plugin internals. The application owns business logic, schedule declarations, Providers, permissions, and deployment timing. Scheduler owns scheduling projections, trigger history, and execution state. The plugin's `skills/` directory is the source of truth; do not edit the synchronized application copy under `.agents/skills/`.

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the task list and detail pages the application builds on the Scheduler API to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

## Then Choose the Execution Model

After deciding to use Scheduler, choose according to business complexity:

| Scenario                                                                                  | Choice          | Implementation boundary                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Periodic cleanup, cache refresh, a single report, or one business Service call            | Own target type | Short operations may complete directly; hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor`                             |
| A simple operation that happens hourly                                                    | Own target type | Cron alone is not a reason to introduce a heavier execution system                                       |
| Immediate asynchronous execution                                                          | `JobExecutor`   | No Cron Schedule is needed                                                                               |
| A one-time delay, such as ten minutes after a request                                     | Queue `delay`   | No Cron Schedule is needed; `JobExecutor` has no delay                                                   |
| Another execution system with its own references, status queries, and completion protocol | Own target type | The same `registerTarget()` call, with `inspect()` and completion reporting for work that finishes later |

Every schedule points at a registered target; `registerTarget()` is the target extension surface, and there is no built-in target type. A target does not guarantee exactly-once external business effects; design business idempotency.

## Read by Task

- To create or change a schedule, read [Definitions, Registration, and Synchronization](references/definitions.md).
- To register or review a target, hand its work to a `JobExecutor`, report completion, or diagnose a historical occurrence after retargeting, read [Target Extensions and Execution Protocol](references/targets.md).
- To integrate or review the UI/API and permissions, interpret status, or diagnose missing definitions, read [Operations and Verification](references/operations.md), including the complete management route table, separate status dimensions, and application identity checks.

## Development Loop

1. Establish which tasks and execution records administrators need to see. Check Server/Client/CLI registration, page permissions, the application's `jobs` configuration (a `redis` adapter for more than one instance; `memory` serves one process) and `JobExecutorServiceProvider` composition and target availability, then choose or register the target type.
2. Implement business logic and Providers in application source, and call `schedulerServiceToken.defineSchedule(definition)` with an application-wide stable key from that Provider's `register()`/`boot()`.
3. Validate payload/input, timezone, idempotency, and the full asynchronous chain: submission, actual job execution, terminal notification, and recovery from persisted execution state. Obtain credentials through secure business Service configuration, never `target.config`.
4. Run application type checks, relevant tests, and build. Synchronize definitions and, in development, use an administrator account to find the task in the UI and track a real execution to its final state. Confirm the business result.
5. Report the schedule key, execution model, timezone, validation evidence, and unverified runtime boundaries. Use `--finalize` in production only when the complete manifest is visible.

原文件 SHA-256：b068028d4f469d254eff36dd9aa38d62674285d25825a2f9c61937b762bb076d

E6 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/targets.md:3–52

目标在 Provider.boot() 注册，validate/start 明确；短任务可 completed，重复 start 需由业务幂等；目标类型用 app.* 命名且重复注册会抛错。

A schedule points at a target, and a target is the plugin's one extension point: `scheduler.registerTarget(target)` declares how a configuration is validated, how a firing starts, and — for work that finishes later — how a run is inspected and reported. Register targets in their owning Provider's `boot()`: all Providers have registered services, and Scheduler has not yet synchronized the manifest in `start()`. Do not resolve the container during module import or depend on another Provider's boot order to create services. Duplicate target types throw.

## Add an Ordinary Scheduled Task

This short task example can be used independently. Create `server/providers/scheduled-log.ts` in the application and add the Provider to its existing Provider array:

```ts
import { loggingToken } from '@nocobase/app-server/logging';
import type { AppPluginApplication } from '@nocobase/app-server/plugins';
import { schedulerServiceToken } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/server/tokens';
import { ServiceProvider } from '@nocobase/service-provider';

export default class ScheduledLogProvider extends ServiceProvider<AppPluginApplication> {
  public readonly name = 'app/scheduled-log';

  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {
    const scheduler = this.app.container.resolve(schedulerServiceToken);
    const logger = this.app.container.resolve(loggingToken).getLogger();
    scheduler.registerTarget({
      type: 'app.scheduled-log',
      title: 'Scheduled time report',
      validate(config) {
        return config !== null &&
          typeof config === 'object' &&
          !Array.isArray(config) &&
          typeof (config as { message?: unknown }).message === 'string'
          ? { valid: true }
          : { valid: false, reason: 'message-must-be-a-string' };
      },
      async start(config, context) {
        logger.info(
          {
            message: config.message,
            scheduleId: context.scheduleId,
            occurrenceId: context.occurrenceId,
          },
          'Scheduled log',
        );
        return { state: 'completed', outcome: 'succeeded' };
      },
    });
  }
}
```

Use target `{ type: 'app.scheduled-log', config: { message: 'Time report' } }`. For business work, replace logging with a domain Service call and validate the complete config. Resolve Scheduler directly when it is required; use `container.has()` to skip registration only for a truly optional integration.

Namespace the type so it cannot collide with another plugin's: `app.` for an application's own tasks, the plugin name for a plugin's. Two registrations of the same type throw at boot.

Short operations may complete inside `start()`, but occupy the schedule worker. Hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor` and return `accepted`, as the next section shows. Do not let config select arbitrary module paths, job names, queue names or channels.

E7 · packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/plugins.js:69–85

defineAppRoutes() 固定 parent='app'，递归冻结 route 的 children/navigation；路由贡献只描述导航元数据，不在其中渲染页面。

export function defineAppRoutes(routes) {
    return Object.freeze({
        parent: 'app',
        routes: freezeNavigationRoutes(routes),
    });
}
function freezeNavigationRoutes(routes) {
    return Object.freeze(routes.map((route) => Object.freeze({
        ...route,
        ...(route.navigation
            ? { navigation: Object.freeze({ ...route.navigation }) }
            : {}),
        ...(route.children
            ? { children: freezeNavigationRoutes(route.children) }
            : {}),
    })));
}

resolveAppClientContributions() 校验每个 route contribution 的 parent，非 app parent 直接抛出 unsupported parent 错误并提示用 defineAppRoutes()。

export function resolveAppClientContributions(contributions) {
    const routeIds = new Map();
    const claimedPaths = new Map();
    const reactProviders = [];
    const reactProviderIds = new Set();
    const inputs = [];
    for (const contribution of contributions) {
        const packageName = normalizePackageName(contribution.packageName);
        const source = normalizeContributionSource(contribution.source);
        const routeContributions = normalizeRouteContributions(contribution.routes);
        for (const routeContribution of routeContributions) {
            if (routeContribution.parent !== 'app') {
                throw new Error(`Plugin "${packageName}" contributed routes to unsupported parent "${String(routeContribution.parent)}"; declare them with defineAppRoutes().`);
            }
            inputs.push({
                definitions: routeContribution.routes,
                packageName,
                source,
            });

E10 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/page.md:97–144

PageContainer/PageHeader 示例导入 Outlet；第 143 行明确“带子路由的页面必须自己放 <Outlet />，否则子路由内容不渲染”，第 140 行区分覆盖式 RouteChildPage。

Put the page in `client/pages/<feature>/index.tsx`, default-export the component, and build its skeleton with `PageContainer` and `PageHeader`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from '@nocobase/i18n/client';
import { PlusIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { Link, Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router';

import { PageContainer } from '#components/page-container';
import { PageHeader } from '#components/page-header';
import { Button } from '#components/ui/button';

export default function ProjectsPage(): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const location = useLocation();

  return (
    <PageContainer>
      <PageHeader
        title={t('projects.title')}
        description={t('projects.description')}
        actions={
          // Create is the child route /projects/new; keep the query parameters so the filters survive closing the dialog.
          <Button
            nativeButton={false}
            render={<Link to={{ pathname: 'new', search: location.search }} />}
          >
            <PlusIcon data-icon='inline-start' />
            {t('projects.create.action')}
          </Button>
        }
      />
      {/* Toolbar, table… */}
      {/* Child routes (create dialog, detail drawer) render here */}
      <Outlet />
    </PageContainer>
  );
}
```

- **`PageContainer`** (`#components/page-container`) renders a `section` that owns the full width, the spacing between blocks and the responsive padding (`w-full space-y-6 p-6 md:p-8`); the back button, the title, actions, content and the loading, empty and error states all go inside it. Do not hand-write an outer `div`, `main` or `section` with page padding, and do not change its spacing on one page (to change it everywhere, change the component).
- **`PageContainer` is provided by the component that owns the page, one per page**:
  - An inline child page (including tab content) renders inside the parent page's `PageContainer`; do not add another one.
  - A covering child page uses its own `PageContainer` inside `RouteChildPage` (see [`child-routes.md`](child-routes.md)). A child route that returns a bare `PageContainer` is neither: it renders at the parent's `Outlet`, below the parent's content.
  - Dialog and drawer content uses the overlay's own container; do not add `PageContainer`.
- **`PageHeader`** (`#components/page-header`) props: `title` (required), `description`, `actions` (on the right, for page-level actions). The title matches the menu name (guidelines L1, L3 and L5).
- A page with child routes must place `<Outlet />` itself, or the child route content does not render; put it at the end of `PageContainer`. For how to write child routes, see [`child-routes.md`](child-routes.md) and [`overlay.md`](overlay.md).
- Navigate to a child route with a relative path (`new`, `id`, `` `edit/${id}` ``) and keep the query string, as [section 2.2 of `overlay.md`](overlay.md#22-place-the-outlet-in-the-parent-page) explains.

原文件 SHA-256：0f94eed27a73f1990ea2ef1e723cdacac3a7117dd8399ace0278febb0e67c2a0

E11 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/child-routes.md:7–17

父页面必须自行放 <Outlet />，框架不会自动插入；裸 PageContainer 会渲染在父内容下方，故独立子页面应使用 RouteChildPage。

## 1. Basic rules

- **Child routes go in the parent route's `children`**, declared in `client/routes.ts`, not in page component files.
- A child route's `path` is relative to its parent and is appended to the parent's path. A leading `/` is stripped before joining, so `new` and `/new` behave the same; this handbook writes the form without `/`.
- **The parent page must place `<Outlet />` itself**, where the child content should appear. Pages do not insert an Outlet automatically, and neither do `RouteChildPage`, `RouteDialog` or `RouteDrawer`; only a plain navigation group gets its Outlet from the route renderer.
- **A child route that is a page of its own returns `RouteChildPage`.** Only tab content renders inline. A child route with a `PageContainer` of its own — a record's page, a form too long for a dialog, an import page — wraps it in `RouteChildPage` ([section 5](#5-covering-child-pages-routechildpage)). Returned bare, it renders at the parent's `Outlet`, below everything the parent shows, and the user has to scroll past the parent page to find it. A page that returns a bare `PageContainer` belongs on a top-level route instead.
- Child routes inherit the entry route's `auth` and cannot change it, and follow the `authz` inheritance rules in [section 4 of `page.md`](page.md#4-authz-page-authorization): a child page renders only after the parent page's check passes.
- Link to a child route with a relative path and keep the query string ([section 2.2 of `overlay.md`](overlay.md#22-place-the-outlet-in-the-parent-page)). A relative path resolves against the route that renders the link, not the URL on screen ([section 2 of `page.md`](page.md#2-the-page-component)).
- Route declarations have no `index` field. Do not invent an index route, and do not register a child route at the parent's own path; when "opening the parent URL shows a particular child page" is needed, use the redirect in [section 4](#4-page-tabs).
- Do not change the shell, the route renderer or the ServiceProvider to add a menu entry; menu entries come only from `navigation` on routes ([section 6 of `page.md`](page.md#6-menus)).
- Design paths around business needs; there is no fixed naming format. Do not write the deployment base path `/main`.

原文件 SHA-256：53387eafc9638904295cd2d711f364fa0f3db48c0963c74ab45fc23199a800da

E12 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/client/auth-provider.js:7–98

PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS=10_000；onOpen 取消定时器并发布 ready，定时器到点则 cancel、unsubscribe 并发布 failed；成功后安装的 onOpen 在超时后已被注销，不会恢复。

const PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
const AuthenticationContext = createContext(undefined);
export function AuthenticationProvider({ children, }) {
    const client = useService(authenticationClientToken);
    const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
    const [isPending, setPendingState] = useState(true);
    const refreshAttemptRef = useRef(0);
    const refresh = async () => {
        const attempt = ++refreshAttemptRef.current;
        setPendingState(true);
        try {
            const result = await client.getSession();
            if (attempt === refreshAttemptRef.current)
                setSession(result.data);
        }
        finally {
            if (attempt === refreshAttemptRef.current)
                setPendingState(false);
        }
    };
    useEffect(() => {
        void refresh();
        return () => {
            refreshAttemptRef.current += 1;
        };
    }, [client]);
    const services = useClientApplication().services;
    const sessionKey = JSON.stringify([
        session?.user.id ?? null,
        session?.session.id ?? null,
    ]);
    const signedIn = session !== null;
    const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
    const retryPreparation = useCallback(() => setRetry((value) => value + 1), []);
    const [prepared, setPrepared] = useState();
    const previousSessionRef = useRef(undefined);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isPending)
            return;
        const realtime = services.has(realtimeClientToken)
            ? services.resolveIfCreated(realtimeClientToken)
            : undefined;
        const changed = previousSessionRef.current !== sessionKey;
        const hadSession = previousSessionRef.current !== undefined;
        previousSessionRef.current = sessionKey;
        if (!signedIn) {
            // Sign-out must also remove the identity from an existing connection.
            if (changed && hadSession)
                realtime?.reconnect();
            return;
        }
        if (!changed &&
            prepared?.sessionKey === sessionKey &&
            prepared.retry === retry &&
            prepared.status === 'ready')
            return;
        const publish = (status) => {
            // Preparation is an external connection lifecycle, independent of rendering.
            // eslint-disable-next-line @eslint-react/set-state-in-effect
            setPrepared({ sessionKey, retry, status });
        };
        if (!realtime?.active) {
            publish('ready');
            return;
        }
        let cancelled = false;
        publish('preparing');
        const unsubscribe = realtime.onOpen(() => {
            // Authorization's synchronous onOpen invalidation must finish first,
            // regardless of listener registration order.
            queueMicrotask(() => {
                if (cancelled)
                    return;
                cancelled = true;
                clearTimeout(timer);
                unsubscribe();
                publish('ready');
            });
        });
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            cancelled = true;
            unsubscribe();
            publish('failed');
        }, PREPARATION_TIMEOUT_MS);
        // Even cookie startup reconnects: connected alone does not prove identity.
        realtime.reconnect();
        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
            clearTimeout(timer);
            unsubscribe();
        };
    }, [isPending, sessionKey, signedIn, services, retry]);

E14 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/client-session-and-pages.md:1–30

# Client session and pages

## Reading the session

`useAuthentication()` works anywhere under the application shell:

```tsx
import { useAuthentication } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/client';

export function Greeting(): ReactElement | null {
  const { session, isPending } = useAuthentication();
  if (isPending || !session) return null;
  return <span>{session.user.name}</span>;
}
```

- `session` is `null` while anonymous; `session.user` has `id`, `name`,
  `email`, `username`, `image`, and `emailVerified`.
- `isPending` is true until the first `getSession()` completes and during any
  `refresh()`. Render nothing or a placeholder while pending; do not treat
  pending as anonymous.
- `refresh()` re-reads the session from the server. Call it after any action
  that changes the session outside the provided hooks.
- `client` is the Better Auth client. Every Better Auth client plugin
  configured in `client/config/auth.ts` is available on it.

The provider does not poll and does not listen for revocation. A session
revoked on the server is noticed at the next `refresh()` or when an API call
returns `401`; treat that `401` as "sign in again", not as an application
error.

E15 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:690–705

normalizeMutationFilter 拒绝 filter 与 all 同时出现，并在非 all 的批量 mutation 没有非空 filter 时抛 INVALID_FILTER，防止空 scope 全表更新。

    async normalizeMutationFilter(collection, filter, context, all, operation) {
        if (all) {
            if (filter !== undefined) {
                invalid('INVALID_FILTER', 'filter and all are mutually exclusive.', {
                    collection: collection.name,
                });
            }
            return this.normalizePolicyMutationScope(collection, operation);
        }
        const normalized = await this.normalizeFilterWithoutPolicy(collection, filter, context);
        const scoped = await this.combineMutationPolicyScope(collection, normalized, operation);
        if (!scoped || scoped.root.items.length === 0) {
            invalid('INVALID_FILTER', 'Bulk mutations require a non-empty filter or all: true.', { collection: collection.name, path: ['filter'] });
        }
        return scoped;
    }

        this.phase = 'booted';

        this.assertPhase('booted');
        await this.runPhase('starting', 'started', (provider) => provider.start());
        this.phase = 'started';

E17 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:248–258

DB Skill 明确 filter/values/select/sort 接受 builder callback 或等价 JSON AST，不接受字符串数组简写；updateMany/deleteMany 拒绝空 scope，all:true 仅用于明确全表。

`filter`, `values`, `select` and `sort` each accept a builder callback or the equivalent serializable JSON AST, and mean the same thing either way. They do not accept a shorthand string array such as `['id', 'name']` or `['-createdAt']`. Field names are Collection logical names, never column names. A write returns a result object — the record is `result.record`, alongside `result.version` and the created relation targets — rather than the record itself. `findMany` is awaited for an array or iterated asynchronously to consume a large result incrementally.

The methods are `findOne`, `findMany`, `count`, `exists`, `aggregate`, `groupBy`, `createOne`, `createMany`, `updateOne`, `updateMany`, `upsertOne`, `deleteOne` and `deleteMany`; `describeMutation` and `validateMutation` report capabilities and pre-validate input, and `withPolicy` returns a scoped Repository.

Boundaries that are easy to get wrong:

- `findOne` takes the first match; `updateOne` and `deleteOne` require exactly one; `upsertOne` requires a unique equality condition.
- `updateMany` and `deleteMany` refuse an empty scope. Pass `all: true` only when the task explicitly calls for every row — never to make an error go away.
- `createMany` and `updateMany` do not accept nested relation writes. `createOne` accepts `create` and `connect` on a relation, not the full set.
- `disconnect` detaches; `delete` removes the target record. A relation target update or delete stays inside the current parent record's scope.
- Repository does not apply the application's permissions. A route handler decides what the caller may see and change _before_ anything reaches `filter`, `values` or `select`. `writePolicy` defaults to `true` for an internal Repository and `false` for one reached from an API route, so a route-facing Repository needs an explicit allow list.

E19 · app/server/providers/overdue-todos.ts:25–74

TodoProvider.boot() 注册 app.check-overdue-todos 目标，start() 调用 todos.markOverdue()；defineSchedule 使用 30 秒 cron、UTC 与空 config，未直接写数据库。

  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.app.container.has(schedulerServiceToken)) return;

    const scheduler = this.app.container.resolve(schedulerServiceToken);
    const todos = this.app.container.resolve(todoServiceToken);

    scheduler.registerTarget({
      type: CHECK_OVERDUE_TODOS_TARGET_TYPE,
      title: 'Check overdue todos',
      validate(config) {
        return config &&
          typeof config === 'object' &&
          !Array.isArray(config) &&
          Object.keys(config).length === 0
          ? { valid: true }
          : { valid: false, reason: 'This task takes no parameters.' };
      },
      async start() {
        const { marked, checkedAt } = await todos.markOverdue();
        return {
          state: 'completed',
          outcome: 'succeeded',
          result: { marked, checkedAt },
        };
      },
      async describe() {
        return {
          targetLabel: 'Check overdue todos',
          description: 'Marks past-due, incomplete todos as expired.',
          state: 'ready',
        };
      },
    });

    scheduler.defineSchedule({
      key: CHECK_OVERDUE_TODOS_SCHEDULE_KEY,
      title: 'Check overdue todos',
      description:
        'Marks past-due todos as expired without changing completed todos.',
      schedule: {
        // A short cycle keeps the result observable in a development check. It remains an ordinary schedule with
        // its own enabled state and execution history in the Scheduler UI.
        cron: '*/30 * * * * *',
        timezone: 'UTC',
      },

E44 · app/client/routing/session-preparation.tsx:17–58

SessionPreparation 在 preparation=failed 时订阅 realtimeClientToken 的 onOpen 并在 1.5s 后自动 retryPreparation；autoRetries=0 时仍显示 Loading，晚到连接可由 onOpen 恢复。

/** How long to wait before retrying a connection the preparation gave up on. */
const AUTO_RETRY_DELAY_MS = 1_500;

/** The first identity handshake must invalidate permissions before pages mount. */
export function SessionPreparation({
  children,
}: PropsWithChildren): ReactElement {
  const { preparation, retryPreparation, client, refresh } =
    useAuthentication();
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { services } = useClientApplication();
  const [signingOut, setSigningOut] = useState(false);
  const [signOutFailed, setSignOutFailed] = useState(false);
  const [autoRetries, setAutoRetries] = useState(0);

  // The authentication provider stops waiting once it reports failure, so a realtime connection that opens after
  // the timeout would leave this screen up until someone presses Retry. Keep listening for that connection, and
  // retry once on our own, so a slow connection returns people to the page they asked for without an extra click.
  // That first retry stays on the loading screen: a connection that was merely late should not look like a failure.
  // Only the retry's own failure is reported, and a connection that opens afterwards still brings the page back.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (preparation !== 'failed') return undefined;
    const realtime = services.has(realtimeClientToken)
      ? services.resolveIfCreated(realtimeClientToken)
      : undefined;
    const unsubscribe = realtime?.onOpen(retryPreparation);
    const timer =
      autoRetries === 0
        ? setTimeout(() => {
            setAutoRetries(1);
            retryPreparation();
          }, AUTO_RETRY_DELAY_MS)
        : undefined;
    return () => {
      if (timer !== undefined) clearTimeout(timer);
      unsubscribe?.();
    };
  }, [autoRetries, preparation, retryPreparation, services]);

  if (preparation === 'ready') return <>{children}</>;
  if (preparation === 'preparing' || autoRetries === 0) {
    return <Loading className='min-h-svh' label={t('auth.preparingSession')} />;

原文件 SHA-256：2b42928006af2f9ef4f9ef14436dca674282a1a0a2c4e28f5d5b83ec7bceffa3

01

启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

页面未出现 Something went wrong、空白页或未处理异常，浏览器 errors 与 console 无新增错误

A1 · 6 条操作 · 6 条观察 · 5 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

实际操作

  1. 使用管理员账号登录，进入 Todos 页面记录三条样例：Prepare the production schedule（2020-01-01，未完成）、Confirm the raw material stock（2020-02-01，已完成）、Inspect the new assembly line（2099-01-01，未完成）
  2. 进入既有 Scheduler 设置页，确认 Check overdue todos 计划，先把启用的开关关闭为 Paused，再重新启用为 Active，页面显示 Next run 为 11:32 PM
  3. 保持页面开启并实际等待定时执行，23:32 后点击 Refresh，Last run 更新为 11:32 PM，Successes 由 25 of 25 变为 26 of 26
  4. 打开 View history，读取 23:32:30 的执行记录为 Succeeded、Attempts 1、Details {"marked":0,"checkedAt":"2026-10-21T23:32:30.080Z"}，并看到更早一次 23:19:30 的 Details 为 {"marked":1,...}
  5. 返回 Todos 页面核对三条记录的 Expired 与 Status 列
  6. 说明：本轮浏览器自动化对 WebSocket 的包装缺少 OPEN/CONNECTING 标准静态常量，曾导致应用实时连接守卫显示 Unable to connect；在页面内恢复标准常量后继续，未修改应用源码、未直接写数据库

观察结果

  1. 重新启用后计划显示 Active，Next run 为 Oct 21, 2026, 11:32 PM，开关为 checked=true
  2. 23:32:30 真实调度执行成功，执行记录为 Succeeded，Details 为 {"marked":0,"checkedAt":"2026-10-21T23:32:30.080Z"}，Last run 同步为 11:32 PM，Successes 从 25 增至 26
  3. 执行历史中 23:19:30 的一次执行明确显示 Details {"marked":1,"checkedAt":"2026-10-21T23:19:30.010Z"}，与唯一过期未完成待办对应；滚动到该记录后新增截图佐证
  4. 执行历史中 23:19:30 的一次执行 Details 为 {"marked":1,...}，说明该计划确实标记过 1 条过期待办；之后多次执行均为 marked:0，未重复新增待办
  5. Todos 最终状态：Prepare the production schedule（过期且未完成）Expired=Yes、Status=Expired；Confirm the raw material stock（已完成）Expired=No、Status=Completed；Inspect the new assembly line（未来到期、未完成）Expired=No、Status=Pending，只有过期且未完成的记录被标记
  6. 页面未出现 Something went wrong、空白页或未处理异常，浏览器 errors 与 console 无新增错误

来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

02

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

页面未出现 Something went wrong、空白页或未处理异常

A2 · 4 条操作 · 4 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

实际操作

  1. 在 B01 之后保持 Check overdue todos 计划为 Active，等待同一计划再发生一次真实定时执行
  2. 23:33 后刷新执行历史，确认新增 23:33:00 的 Succeeded 记录，Details {"marked":0,"checkedAt":"2026-10-21T23:33:00.006Z"}
  3. 进入 Todos 页面，统计表格数据行数量并用 Refresh 重新读取页面状态
  4. 核对三条记录的 Expired 与 Status 是否与 B01 一致

观察结果

  1. 执行历史新增 23:33:00 Succeeded，Attempts 1，marked:0；随后 23:33:30 又执行一次仍为 Succeeded、marked:0，说明重复执行同一目标均正常返回
  2. Todos 表格仍为 3 条数据行（document.querySelectorAll('tbody tr').length = 3），待办总数没有增加
  3. 三条状态保持正确：过期未完成 Expired=Yes/Expired；已完成 Expired=No/Completed；未来未完成 Expired=No/Pending
  4. 页面未出现 Something went wrong、空白页或未处理异常

来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

03

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

本项未扩展验证长期停用期间不会执行，仅确认配置持久化；页面无 Something went wrong 或未处理异常

A3 · 5 条操作 · 4 条观察 · 3 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

实际操作

  1. 进入既有 Scheduler 设置页，确认 Check overdue todos 当前为 Active，开关 aria 名称为 Disable Check overdue todos 且 checked=true
  2. 点击该开关停用计划，页面显示 Paused、Next run 为 —，开关变为 Enable Check overdue todos 且 checked=false
  3. 对当前设置页执行完整浏览器刷新（reload）
  4. 刷新后因自动化工具 WebSocket 包装缺陷出现连接守卫页，恢复标准 WebSocket 常量并点击 Retry 重新进入设置页，读取计划配置
  5. 仅评估停用配置在刷新后的持久化，未声称验证长期停用效果

观察结果

  1. 停用前：Active，Next run 为 11:34 PM，开关 checked=true
  2. 停用后：Paused，Next run 为 —，开关 aria 名称为 Enable Check overdue todos 且 checked=false
  3. 完整刷新并重新进入设置页后：仍为 Paused，Next run 为 —，开关仍为 checked=false，说明停用状态已保存到服务端并在刷新后保持
  4. 本项未扩展验证长期停用期间不会执行，仅确认配置持久化；页面无 Something went wrong 或未处理异常

来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

完整原始验收要求
B01. 启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

B02. 再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

B03. 在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。

无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。
